Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The isotopes operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 898. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 854 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on isotopes structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 898. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 854. QED.

Theorem 219.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For optimality condition pairs (A,B) in isotopes theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 303 and the invariant I(A,B) = 370 mod 3.

For the inductive step, we decompose the isotopes structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 370. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
